# Tokens reference

> **Generated reference — do not edit by hand.** This page is emitted by
> `packages/tokens/scripts/generate-reference.mjs` from the DTCG sources in
> `packages/tokens/tokens/`. Regenerate it after changing token sources with
> `pnpm --filter @kimen/tokens run reference` and commit the result. It is
> generated manually and is not verified by a CI gate.

Every custom property in the public styling contract: 550 semantic
and 2418 component tokens. Values are the resolved **onmars light**
values; dark mode and the material3 theme reassign the same custom properties,
so the names and descriptions on this page apply to every theme (see
*Docs → Tokens & theming* for the model). Primitive tokens are internal and
deliberately not listed: components never consume them, and reassigning the
two layers below is the whole re-theming surface.

## Semantic tokens

Meaning, not appearance. This is the layer a brand reassigns to re-theme
every component at once.

### `ki.activity-size`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-activity-size-dot` | `0.375rem` | Diameter of a typing dot at its full size. |
| `--ki-activity-size-ring-lg` | `2rem` | Large inline activity ring diameter. |
| `--ki-activity-size-ring-md` | `1.25rem` | Medium inline activity ring diameter. |
| `--ki-activity-size-ring-sm` | `1rem` | Small inline activity ring diameter. |
| `--ki-activity-size-stroke` | `2px` | Stroke of a small or medium activity ring. |
| `--ki-activity-size-stroke-lg` | `3px` | Stroke of a large activity ring. |

| `--ki-anchored-surface-border-width` | `1px` | Outline thickness for a small anchored surface. |

| --- | --- | --- |

### `ki.density`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-density-control-offset` | `0rem` | Added to the block size of every scaled control and row (button, icon button, field, select, textarea, option, list item, tab). Components floor the result at ki.size.target-min. |
| `--ki-density-space-scale` | `1` | Multiplies every scaled container padding and gap (card, dialog, alert, list, list item, select listbox, tab panel, tooltip). |
| `--ki-density-target-min` | `24px` | The smallest pointer target any density may produce: 24 x 24 CSS px by default and in compact (WCAG 2.5.8 AA), 44 x 44 in touch (WCAG 2.5.5). It floors a shrinking control but never lifts what a control paints: a control painted smaller extends its target to it, invisibly; rows and tabs grow to it (spec 045 A1). |

### `ki.effect`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-effect-component-backdrop-blur` | `0px` | Deprecated since 0.1.0, removed in 1.0.0. Use --ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter. MarsUI Blur/24 as the component backdrop; none in onmars v2. Legacy blur radius for the shared backdrop filter. |
| `--ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete backdrop filter for translucent components. Use none to turn the effect off. |
| `--ki-effect-component-listbox-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete listbox backdrop filter. The stronger onmars v1 blur; flat themes can turn it off. |
| `--ki-effect-component-menu-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Optional complete backdrop filter for a raised action menu. |
| `--ki-effect-component-no-backdrop-filter` | `none` | An unadorned surface applies no backdrop filter. |
| `--ki-effect-component-primary-default-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | MarsUI Component_effect/primary_default: drop 0/1/1/-0.5 plus inner White/12 0/3/3. Pair with --ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter — CSS cannot mix backdrop blur into box-shadow. |
| `--ki-effect-component-primary-hover-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | MarsUI Component_effect/primary_hover: drops 0/20/20/-12 + 0/3/3/-1.5 + 0/1/1/-0.5 plus inner White/18 0/3/3. Pair with --ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter. |
| `--ki-effect-component-secondary-default-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | MarsUI Component_effect/secondary_default: drop 0/2/1.5/-0.5 plus inner White/3 0/2/3. Pair with --ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter. |
| `--ki-effect-component-secondary-hover-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | MarsUI Component_effect/secondary_hover: drops as primary_hover plus inner White/8 0/2/3. Pair with --ki-effect-component-backdrop-filter. |
| `--ki-effect-no-backdrop-filter` | `none` | A surface that paints without filtering its backdrop. |
| `--ki-effect-small-dark-shadow` | `0 2px 3px -1.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.32)` | MarsUI Small_dark_shadow: drop 0/2/3/-1.5 in Elevation/shadow_dark (Black/32 light, Black/48 dark). Lifts small light glyphs off saturated fills — the radio selected dot and the switch pointer carry it in the masters. |
| `--ki-effect-video-hover-opacity` | `1` | Opacity of the added play-container shadow on hover. onmars shows the full primary hover effect over the resting shadow. |

### `ki.elevation`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-elevation-e1` | `0 1px 2px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.05)` | MarsUI Elevation/e1. Scheme-aware: every layer colors through ki.elevation.shadow. |
| `--ki-elevation-e2` | `0 1px 2px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.05), 0 4px 10px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.06), 0 3px 8px 0 transparent` | MarsUI Elevation/e2 (cumulative: e1 layers plus 0/3/3/-1.5). |
| `--ki-elevation-e3` | `0 2px 6px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.05), 0 12px 28px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.09), 0 10px 24px 0 transparent` | MarsUI Elevation/e3 (cumulative: e2 layers plus 0/20/20/-12). |
| `--ki-elevation-e4` | `0 4px 12px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.07), 0 20px 44px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.12), 0 18px 40px 0 transparent` | MarsUI Elevation/e4 (cumulative: e3 layers plus 0/32/32/-16). |
| `--ki-elevation-e5` | `0 1px 1px -0.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 3px 3px -1.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 20px 20px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 32px 32px -16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 56px 56px -28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)` | MarsUI Elevation/e5 (cumulative: e4 layers plus 0/56/56/-28). |
| `--ki-elevation-e6` | `0 1px 1px -0.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 3px 3px -1.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 20px 20px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 32px 32px -16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 56px 56px -28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 72px 72px -36px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)` | MarsUI Elevation/e6 (cumulative: e5 layers plus 0/72/72/-36). |
| `--ki-elevation-none` | `0 0 #0000` | Flat surfaces carry no resting shadow. |
| `--ki-elevation-shadow` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)` | semantic color for elevation shadow. |
| `--ki-elevation-shadow-dark` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.32)` | semantic color for elevation shadow dark. |
| `--ki-elevation-shadow-med` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)` | semantic color for elevation shadow med. |

### `ki.focus`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-focus-danger` | `0 0 0 3px rgba(239, 78, 105, 0.2)` | MarsUI Focus/danger: persistent 3px spread ring of Outline/danger_base_em_alpha (danger-500 at 20% light / 40% dark) — the danger state signal on Input_field and Dropdown masters (measured rgb(252,220,219) over white). Pairs with a white fill and hairline border; Kimen keeps its opaque danger border on top as a declared a11y deviation. |
| `--ki-focus-gray` | `0 0 0 3px rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.16)` | MarsUI Focus/gray: 3px spread focus ring of Outline/med_em (scheme-aware via the outline token). |
| `--ki-focus-primary` | `0 0 0 3px rgba(188, 76, 38, 0.2)` | MarsUI Focus/primary: 3px spread focus ring of Outline/primary_base_em_alpha, read from the Figma master as #845abe33 = brand 500 at 20% (Kimen name: outline.primary-base-em-alpha / brand.500-alpha-2). The previous 40% (outline.primary-low-em-alpha / brand.500-alpha-4) came from specs/002-ki-button/design-extraction.md §2.5, which read the alpha off the Dark canvas mode and recorded it as the Light value; the ring shipped 2x too strong in light and 2.8x in dark across twelve components. |
| `--ki-focus-ring-color` | `#bb4e2a` | Colour of the keyboard focus ring every focusable component draws: 3:1 or more against the page in every theme and scheme. |
| `--ki-focus-ring-offset` | `0.125rem` | Gap between a focusable component and its keyboard focus ring. |
| `--ki-focus-ring-width` | `0.125rem` | Thickness of the keyboard focus ring every focusable component draws (its outline). One token instead of one per component, so a mode or a theme changes every ring at once. |

### `ki.outline`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |

| `--ki-outline-base-em` | `rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.1)` | semantic color for outline base em. |
| `--ki-outline-black` | `#000000` | semantic color for outline black. |
| `--ki-outline-control` | `#86827b` | The boundary of a control that can be EMPTY — field, checkbox, radio, switch. Such a control carries no label inside its own box, so the box IS what identifies it and must clear WCAG 1.4.11's 3:1 against every surface it can sit on (s0-s2). Pinned to an accessibility floor, NOT to a position in the emphasis ramp: rebalancing outline.base/low/med/high must never silently erase a control's edge. MarsUI draws Outline/Black-8 here, but as an edge over a filled cell on a fixed canvas — transcribed literally it measured 1.19:1 against a fill identical to the page (audit 2026-07-25). Declared deviation. |

The mirror of ki.state-layer.pressed, which is the next rung above hover-overlay and washes the other way. Two conventions exist because ki-button paints primary on a dark fill and secondary on a light one; a single pressed overlay would lighten one of them on press and read as less pressed, not more. |

| `--ki-text-danger` | `#c21a45` | Deprecated alias kept for pre-Fase-1 consumers; use ki.text.danger-high-em, the danger ink that reads as text (4.9:1 or more on s0-s2). danger-med-em is under 4.5:1 on the light surfaces. |

| `--ki-text-danger-low-em` | `#b84455` | semantic color for text danger low em. The shared ramp step reads under 4.5:1 on the light surfaces (s0-s2), so onmars v2 and Material 3 light set a readable ink of the same hue: 4.5:1 or more on s0-s2 in both schemes (gate: check-contrast). A theme that keeps the ramp step uses it for large text (24px, or 18.66px bold), icons and decoration only. |

The shared ramp step reads under 4.5:1 on the light surfaces (s0-s2), so onmars v2 and Material 3 light set a darker ink of the same hue that reaches 4.5:1 or more on s0-s2 in both schemes (gate: check-contrast). ## Component tokens

The public styling contract of each element. Reassign them on `:root` for a
theme-wide change, on a theme root (an element with `data-ki-theme`) for a
region, or on one element for that instance. A token that another token
aliases is recomputed only on the root and on theme roots.

### `ki-accordion`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-accordion-divider-width` | `1px` | Width of the separator between accordion rows. |

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-ai-label-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete filter behind the AI provenance mark. Defaults to none. |
| `--ki-ai-label-bg` | `#ffebe5` | Fill of the AI provenance mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-bg-image` | `none` | Optional image over the AI provenance fill. Defaults to none. |
| `--ki-ai-label-border-width` | `1px` | Border width of the panel and the forced-colors mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-fg` | `#9a3715` | Text and icon ink of the AI provenance mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-gap` | `0.25rem` | Gap between the mark and its icon or between provenance metadata items. |
| `--ki-ai-label-md-height` | `1.5rem` | Minimum painted block size of the medium AI mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-md-icon-size` | `1rem` | Sparkles icon size in the medium AI mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-md-padding-inline` | `0.5rem` | Inline inset of the medium AI mark. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete filter behind the provenance explanation. Defaults to none. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-bg` | `#f9f7f4` | Raised fill of the provenance explanation. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-bg-image` | `none` | Optional image over the provenance explanation fill. Defaults to none. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-border` | `rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.1)` | Hairline color of the explanation panel. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-fg` | `#181613` | Ink of the provenance explanation and metadata. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-gap` | `0.5rem` | Gap between the panel sections and between the panel and trigger. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-padding` | `0.75rem` | Inset of the explanation panel. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-radius` | `12px` | Corner radius of the explanation panel. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-shadow` | `0 2px 6px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.05), 0 12px 28px 0 rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.09), 0 10px 24px 0 transparent` | Elevation of the explanation panel. |
| `--ki-ai-label-popover-width` | `18rem` | Preferred inline size of the explanation panel. |
| `--ki-ai-label-radius` | `6px` | Corner radius of the AI provenance mark. |

### `ki-alert`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-alert-actions-gap` | `0.75rem` | dimension used by the alert component for the gap between slotted actions (MarsUI Alert master CTAs gap Space/xl 12px). |
| `--ki-alert-border-width` | `1px` | dimension used by the alert component for the card border width (the MarsUI Alert master card stroke is fully transparent). onmars v2 draws a hairline (spec 011 A2); material3 keeps its 2px border via its override. |
| `--ki-alert-card-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | shadow used by the alert component for the card elevation (MarsUI Alert master List_wrap: elevation ramp level e5). An inline alert does not float, so onmars v2 and material3 lay the card flat. |
| `--ki-alert-danger-bg` | `#ffebeb` | color used by the alert component for the danger card background (MarsUI Alert master: the List_wrap card is Surface/special/light-s0-dark-s2 in every tone). onmars v2 tints the card with surface.danger-tint so the tone reads on the whole card (spec 011 A2); other themes may tint it per tone here too. |
| `--ki-alert-danger-border` | `#ffcccf` | color used by the alert component for the danger card edge (MarsUI draws no tone border on any alert/note/toast surface). onmars v2 draws the outline.danger-low-em hairline around the tinted card (spec 011 A2). |
| `--ki-alert-danger-fg` | `#181613` | color used by the alert component for the danger card foreground: heading and message ink (MarsUI Alert master Text/high-em in every tone; the message keeps this 4.5:1 ink instead of the master Text/low-em, spec 011 A1-D8). |
| `--ki-alert-danger-icon-bg` | `#ffebeb` | color used by the alert component for the danger icon frame (MarsUI Alert master Feature-icon Surface/danger-base-em-alpha at 12%). |
| `--ki-alert-danger-icon-fg` | `#c21a45` | color used by the alert component for the danger icon glyph (MarsUI Alert master Feature-icon glyph Text/danger-high-em). |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-active-fg` | `#181613` | color used by the alert component for dismiss active foreground. |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete backdrop filter behind the alert dismiss button. |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-border-width` | `1px` | dimension used by the alert component for the dismiss chip outline (MarsUI 24px circle: 1px Outline/secondary_button_top hairline). |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-hover-bg` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03)` | color used by the alert component for dismiss hover background (MarsUI hover mechanism: hover-overlay-inverse Black/3 wash). |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-hover-fg` | `#181613` | color used by the alert component for dismiss hover foreground. |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-icon-size` | `1rem` | dimension used by the alert component for dismiss icon size (Figma close glyph box 16px inside the 24px raised circle; repointed from 18px per the fidelity audit). |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the alert component for the dismiss chip shape (MarsUI Radius/radius_round full circle). |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-rest-fg` | `#181613` | color used by the alert component for dismiss rest foreground. |
| `--ki-alert-dismiss-size` | `1.5rem` | dimension used by the alert component for dismiss size. |
| `--ki-alert-focus-ring-color` | `#bb4e2a` | color used by the alert component for focus ring color. |
| `--ki-alert-focus-ring-offset` | `0.125rem` | dimension used by the alert component for focus ring offset. |
| `--ki-alert-focus-ring-shadow` | `0 0 0 3px rgba(188, 76, 38, 0.2)` | shadow used by the alert dismiss control for the focus ring glow (MarsUI Focus/primary: 3px spread ring at 20% — see ki.focus.primary. specs/002-ki-button/design-extraction.md §2.5 recorded 40%, having read the alpha off the Dark canvas mode, same double mechanism as ki-button: glow plus the opaque indicator). |
| `--ki-alert-focus-ring-width` | `0.125rem` | dimension used by the alert component for focus ring width. |
| `--ki-alert-font-size` | `0.8125rem` | dimension used by the alert component for font size. |
| `--ki-alert-gap` | `0.75rem` | dimension used by the alert component for the card stack gap between icon, text and actions (MarsUI Alert master List_wrap gap Space/xl 12px). |

| `--ki-alert-heading-font-size` | `0.9375rem` | dimension used by the alert component for heading font size. |
| `--ki-alert-heading-font-weight` | `600` | font weight used by the alert component for heading font weight (MarsUI UI/Body 2/medium: weight 600 on the master heading). |
| `--ki-alert-icon-glyph-size` | `1.25rem` | dimension used by the alert component for the icon glyph inside the frame (MarsUI Alert master Feature-icon glyph 20px). |
| `--ki-alert-icon-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the alert component for the icon frame radius (MarsUI Alert master Feature-icon: a circle). |
| `--ki-alert-icon-size` | `2rem` | dimension used by the alert component for the icon frame (MarsUI Alert master Feature-icon size xs: 32px). |

| `--ki-alert-neutral-fg` | `#181613` | color used by the alert component for the neutral card foreground: heading and message ink (MarsUI Alert master Text/high-em in every tone; the message keeps this 4.5:1 ink instead of the master Text/low-em, spec 011 A1-D8). |

Use --ki-alert-shell-backdrop-filter. backdrop blur used by the alert component behind the shell (MarsUI Alert master BACKGROUND_BLUR Blur/24 = CSS 12px; reduced transparency and material3 resolve to blur none through the semantic token). |

### `ki-avatar`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-avatar-bg` | `#ebe7e1` | color used by the avatar component for the initials/figure surface: Surface/primary_med_em, the exact variable behind the Type=text gradient start and the Type=icon solid fill. The Figma text gradient (primary_med_em -> primary_low_em_alpha 20%) is deliberately flattened to its solid start color — the token pipeline models no gradients and the spec defines one family-level bg (declared deviation, specs/019-ki-avatar/design-extraction.md decision 2, pending founder ratification). onmars v2 fills it with the neutral surface.s3, since a list of people is not a list of selected items. |
| `--ki-avatar-border-color` | `rgba(24, 22, 19, 0.1)` | color used by the avatar component for the surface border: Outline/base_em (Black/3 light, White/3 dark), the exact variable on Type=text variants. |
| `--ki-avatar-border-width` | `1px` | dimension used by the avatar component for the surface border (MarsUI 1px hairline on Type=text; applied to the token surface behind initials and figure, dropped under a loaded portrait — design-extraction decision 4). |
| `--ki-avatar-fg` | `#5e5953` | color used by the avatar component for the initials text and the generic-figure glyph (via currentColor): Text/primary_on_primary, the exact variable on every Type=text variant. onmars v2 sets the initials in the text.supporting ink (med-em, stronger under more contrast) on its neutral fill. |
| `--ki-avatar-font-family` | `'Instrument Sans', system-ui, sans-serif` | font family used by the avatar component for initials: the body family (Instrument Sans in onmars v2). |
| `--ki-avatar-font-weight` | `600` | font weight used by the avatar component for initials (MarsUI Typeface/weight/body/medium: 600). |
| `--ki-avatar-lg-font-size` | `0.9375rem` | dimension used by the avatar component for lg initials (MarsUI UI/Body 2: 15px). |
| `--ki-avatar-lg-icon-size` | `1.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar component for the lg generic-figure glyph (MarsUI Icon/User 24px, Space/7xl). |
| `--ki-avatar-lg-size` | `3rem` | dimension used by the avatar component for the lg box (48px circle, Figma Space/14xl). |
| `--ki-avatar-md-font-size` | `0.8125rem` | dimension used by the avatar component for md initials (MarsUI UI/Para: body_1 13px). |

| `--ki-avatar-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the avatar component for the shape: Radius/radius_round bound to every avatar variant — the circle is a radius token, never an attribute (002 shape precedent, FR-005). |

| `--ki-avatar-xs-size` | `1.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar component for the xs box (24px circle, Figma Space/7xl). |

| `--ki-avatar-group-ring-width` | `0rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the separating ring around stacked members: NO Avatar_group frame draws a ring (verified across all five sizes), so onmars resolves the capability to zero — the ring stays a theme decision, never an attribute (FR-008). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-sm-counter-font-size` | `0.8125rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the sm counter text (body_1 13px on the 32px frame — one step above the sm initials, verified). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-sm-counter-padding-inline` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the sm counter inline padding (Space/md 8px on the 32px frame). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-sm-overlap` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the sm member overlap (the exact -8px on the 32px Avatar_group frame 10087:2601). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xl-counter-font-size` | `1.125rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xl counter text (title_2 18px on the 56px frame). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xl-counter-padding-inline` | `0.875rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xl counter inline padding (Space/2xl 14px on the 56px frame). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xl-overlap` | `0.75rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xl member overlap (the exact -12px on the 56px Avatar_group frame 10090:2973). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xs-counter-font-size` | `0.6875rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xs counter text (synthesized 24px step following the avatar xs initials size, declared for founder review). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xs-counter-padding-inline` | `0.375rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xs counter inline padding (synthesized 24px step extending the verified 8/10/12/14 ramp, declared for founder review). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xs-overlap` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xs member overlap: the 24px group step exists in no Figma frame (five-step set), so it inherits the -8px overlap of the neighboring 20/32px frames (synthesized step, spec 019 Assumptions). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xxs-counter-font-size` | `0.6875rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xxs counter text: no counter exists on the 20px frame, so the value follows the avatar xxs initials step (synthesized, declared for founder review). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xxs-counter-padding-inline` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for xxs counter padding inline (raised to clear the 8px member tuck so the + glyph is never occluded; the 20px Figma frame ships no counter — synthesized step, founder review pending). |
| `--ki-avatar-group-xxs-overlap` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the avatar group component for the xxs member overlap (the -8px stack step on the 20px Avatar_group frame — named xs in Figma, the vocabulary skew recorded in spec 019 Assumptions). |

### `ki-indicator`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-block-size` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the resting dot block size: the 8px circle diameter measured in the State=inactive vector (the 16px frame height is row alignment, carried by the current dot). |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-color` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.48)` | color used by the indicator component for the resting dot fill: Inverse_white/alpha_48 (Black/48 light, White/48 dark) — a declared a11y deviation from the MarsUI binding Inverse_white/alpha_18, which reads 1.53:1 light and 1.64:1 dark against the page; alpha_48 is the first step of the same ramp that clears the 3:1 FR-012 and WCAG 1.4.11 ask. The scheme flip rides the semantic layer. |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-current-block-size` | `1rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the current dot block size: Space/3xl, the exact variable bound as the 16px height of the State=active pill (the full row line; declared family extension, design-extraction §Decisiones). |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-current-color` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.48)` | color used by the indicator component for the current dot treatment: Inverse_white/alpha_48, the same step as the resting dots — the MarsUI State=active ring vector binds the variable of the resting fill, and its alpha_18 reads 1.53-1.64:1 against the page (declared a11y deviation, FR-012). Painted as the pill ring in onmars, where elongation carries the state; as the solid dot in themes that zero the hole. |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-current-hole-block-size` | `0.375rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the block size of the transparent hole cut from the current pill: the 6px cutout height measured in the State=active vector; the ring width derives by construction as (current block size − hole block size) / 2 and a theme resolves it to zero for a solid dot (declared family extension, design-extraction §Decisiones). |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-current-inline-size` | `2rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the current dot inline size: Space/10xl, the exact variable bound as the 32px width of the State=active pill — the MarsUI elongation lives here, never in an attribute (FR-007). |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-current-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the indicator component for the current dot shape: Radius/radius_round bound in Figma — the pill is a radius token, never an attribute (002 shape precedent). |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-inline-size` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the resting dot inline size: Space/md, the exact variable bound as the 8px width of the State=inactive symbol; the painted circle is 8x8 centered in the 16px row line. |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the indicator component for the resting dot shape: Radius/radius_round bound in Figma — the round dot is a radius token, never an attribute (002 shape precedent). |
| `--ki-indicator-gap` | `0.375rem` | dimension used by the indicator component for the space between dots: Space/sm, the exact variable bound as the 6px gap of the composed Nav_indicator row (node 14195:5866, specs/024-ki-indicator/design-extraction.md). |
| `--ki-indicator-motion-duration` | `150ms` | duration used by the indicator component for the highlight transfer between dots: the Figma set documents no motion, so onmars resolves the single transition to the fast semantic duration (FR-008; disabled under reduced motion by construction). |
| `--ki-indicator-motion-easing` | `cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1)` | easing used by the indicator component for the highlight-transfer transition (MarsUI standard curve; previously the UA default ease applied). |

| `--ki-qr-color` | `#181613` | color used by the qr component for module/finder ink (the master binds Text/high_em, whose light-scheme hue is Gray/950 #0a0c11; referenced as the scheme-stable primitive because FR-010 keeps QR ink from flipping in dark — 18.9:1 on white). |
| `--ki-qr-finder-radius` | `0px` | dimension used by the qr component for the corner radius of the three finder rings, applied to the ring's midline in the code's internal coordinate space of 8px per module: Type=square renders square concentric finders (radius 0); the documented round value is 14px (14px midline = 18px outer edge, the 2.25-module radius measured on Type=round); the inner-edge and center radii derive by construction (stroke geometry and a 3/7 scale — design-extraction §5). |
| `--ki-qr-module-radius` | `0px` | dimension used by the qr component for the corner radius of every data module, in the code's internal coordinate space of 8px per module: Type=square (the set default) renders pure squares (radius 0); the documented round value is 4px (4px = half a module, the MarsUI Type=round dot carried as a token value, never an attribute — FR-007, 002 shape precedent). |
| `--ki-qr-quiet-zone` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the qr component for the light quiet zone around the modules: the 8px band measured on both 128px variants (about two modules of the sample matrices), painted as tile padding and preserved at any token-driven size (FR-008). |
| `--ki-qr-radius` | `0px` | dimension used by the qr component for the tile corners: the QR_code frames are square with no own radius (the rounded showcase card beneath is framing, not anatomy — design-extraction §1). |
| `--ki-qr-size` | `8rem` | dimension used by the qr component for size (MarsUI 128x128 symbol frame via the dedicated ki.size.qr surface size; replaces the 120px space-scale approximation). |

| `--ki-scroller-focus-ring-shadow` | `0 0 0 3px rgba(188, 76, 38, 0.2)` | shadow used by the scroller component for the viewport focus ring glow (MarsUI Focus/primary 3px 20%-alpha halo, the ki-button double mechanism; completes the theming hook ki-scroller.css already consumes). |

| `--ki-scroller-surface` | `rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)` | color used by the scroller component for the viewport surface: transparent in onmars — the scroller shows the consumer's own surface; a theme may resolve a proper surface here (FR-007). |
| `--ki-scroller-thumb-color` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.48)` | color used by the scroller component for the indicator thumb: Inverse_white/alpha_48 (Black/48 light, White/48 dark) — a declared a11y deviation from the MarsUI binding Inverse_white/alpha_6 (Black/5 light, White/5 dark), which reads 1.12:1 and 1.09:1 against the content surface. The thumb is the only mark that shows the scroll position, so it takes the first step of the same ramp that clears WCAG 1.4.11's 3:1. The scheme flip rides the semantic layer (specs/023-ki-scroller/design-extraction.md). |
| `--ki-scroller-thumb-min-length` | `1rem` | dimension used by the scroller component for the minimum thumb length along the scroll axis: the 16px min-height on the Figma scroll pill; the actual length stays the native visible-fraction proportion (FR-003). |
| `--ki-scroller-thumb-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the scroller component for the thumb shape: Radius/radius_round bound in Figma — the pill is a radius token, never an attribute (002 shape precedent). |
| `--ki-scroller-thumb-thickness` | `0.25rem` | dimension used by the scroller component for the thumb cross size: the 4px pill measured inside the 8px MarsUI rail (rail = thumb + 2×gutter, verified 2026-07-17). |
| `--ki-scroller-track-color` | `rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)` | color used by the scroller component for the track: the MarsUI pill floats directly on the content surface with no visible track — transparent in onmars; a theme opts into a visible track through this token, never an attribute. |
| `--ki-scroller-track-inset` | `0.25rem` | dimension used by the scroller component for the rail end insets: Space/xs bound as the 4px padding along the scroll axis of both Scroller variants (design-extraction §Decisiones 5). |

### `ki-status`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-status-danger-color` | `#ef4e69` | color used by the status component for the danger tone fill: Surface/danger_med_em, the exact variable bound to the Type=danger Status variant (Danger/500). |
| `--ki-status-info-color` | `#0f94bd` | color used by the status component for the info tone fill: Surface/info_high_em (Info/600 light, Info/400 dark) — one step above the med-em 500 pattern the success and danger tones follow, because Info/500 reads 2.29:1 on the light page and WCAG 1.4.11 asks 3:1 of the dot (FR-008). No Figma Status variant ships an info Type, so the fill remains an extrapolation onto the Info ramp (001 contract, 010/015 precedent) declared for founder ratification. |
| `--ki-status-neutral-color` | `#86827b` | color used by the status component for the neutral tone fill: ki.outline.control, the semantic 3:1 non-text floor (Black/48 light, White/48 dark; md.sys.color.outline in material3) — a declared a11y deviation from the MarsUI binding Surface/disabled_high_em (Gray/500 light, White/32 dark), which reads 2.10:1 and 2.78:1 against the page where WCAG 1.4.11 asks 3:1 of the dot (FR-008). MarsUI's disabled gray still maps to the charter neutral tone (spec Assumptions). |
| `--ki-status-radius` | `1000px` | dimension used by the status component for the dot shape: Radius/radius_round bound in Figma — the round dot is a radius token, never an attribute (002 shape precedent). |
| `--ki-status-ring-color` | `#ffffff` | color used by the status component for the separating ring: Outline/inverse_black, the exact variable bound to Outline=True variants — white over light surfaces, black over dark ones, so the ring reads as a cutout from the underlying media (FR-002). |
| `--ki-status-ring-width` | `0.125rem` | dimension used by the status component for the separating ring: the 2px stroke on every Outline=True Status variant (FR-002). |
| `--ki-status-shadow` | `0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | shadow used by the status component for the dot effects: the exact MarsUI pair on every Status variant — e1 drop (0/1/1/-0.5 in the scheme shadow color) plus inner White/12 highlight (0/3/3), i.e. Component_effect/primary_default's box-shadow layers, flat in onmars v2. Its backdrop blur is not applied: a 4px dot has nothing to blur (brief §5 perf note). |
| `--ki-status-size` | `0.5rem` | dimension used by the status component for the dot: the MarsUI 4px dot, Space/xs bound to every Status variant (specs/021-ki-status/design-extraction.md). One size per theme, never an attribute (FR-006). |
| `--ki-status-success-color` | `#409b3f` | color used by the status component for the success tone fill: Surface/success_med_em, the exact variable bound to the Type=success Status variant (Success/500). |
| `--ki-status-warning-color` | `#945b05` | color used by the status component for the warning tone fill: Text/warning_high_em (Warning/800 light, Warning/300 dark) — a declared a11y deviation from the MarsUI binding Surface/warning_med_em (Warning/500), which reads 1.92:1 on the light page. No warning surface step clears the 3:1 WCAG 1.4.11 asks of the dot (Warning/600 reads 2.33:1), so the fill takes the warning ink that is legible on the page (FR-008). |

| `--ki-switch-control-width` | `3rem` | dimension used by the switch component for the control footprint width (Toggle md 10023:1368: Space/14xl 48px). The well is centred in it; set it to the track width, with control-height, to drop the well. |

### `ki-video`

| Token | Value (onmars light) | Description |

| `--ki-video-backdrop-blur` | `0px` | Deprecated since 0.1.0, removed in 1.0.0. Use --ki-video-backdrop-filter. dimension used by the video component for the backdrop of the scrim and the play container (MarsUI Blur/24, as buttons; none in onmars v2 and material3). |
| `--ki-video-backdrop-filter` | `none` | Complete backdrop filter behind the video play scrim and control. |
| `--ki-video-focus-ring-color` | `#bb4e2a` | color used by the video component for the play control focus ring: the keyboard-reachable facade control (FR-004, S6) adopts the ki-dialog/ki-icon-button focus-ring pair verbatim. |
| `--ki-video-focus-ring-offset` | `0.125rem` | dimension used by the video component for the play control focus ring offset (ki-dialog precedent). |
| `--ki-video-focus-ring-width` | `0.125rem` | dimension used by the video component for the play control focus ring width (ki-dialog precedent). |
| `--ki-video-frame-radius` | `16px` | dimension used by the video component for the frame corners: Radius/big_component/radius_xs, the exact variable bound to the Size=md Video frame (node 12089:6570, specs/025-ki-video/design-extraction.md) — the sm frame's radius_md is the scale demo, not a second API value. |
| `--ki-video-motion-duration` | `150ms` | duration used by the video component for the facade dismissal fade: the Figma set documents no motion, so onmars resolves the single transition to the fast semantic duration (FR-013; disabled under reduced motion by construction, 024 precedent). |
| `--ki-video-play-bg` | `#000000` | color used by the video component for the play container surface: Surface/inverse_white, the exact variable bound to the Icon_button fill (black over light, white over dark) — the control carries its own surface so glyph contrast never depends on the poster imagery (FR-008). |
| `--ki-video-play-border-color` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | color used by the video component for the play container edge: Outline/primary_button_top, the exact variable bound to the Icon_button stroke (as ki-button; none in onmars v2). |
| `--ki-video-play-border-width` | `1px` | dimension used by the video component for the play container edge width (MarsUI hairline stroke, ki-icon-button precedent). |
| `--ki-video-play-fg` | `#ffffff` | color used by the video component for the play glyph: Text/inverse_black, the counterpart of the inverse container surface (white over light, black over dark) — the glyph inherits it via currentColor; the -bg/-fg pair is swept by the contrast gate (FR-008). |
| `--ki-video-play-icon-size` | `1.75rem` | dimension used by the video component for the play glyph: the 28px Icon/Placeholder_xl slot measured inside the Size=md play container (the ki-icon-button xl icon size). |
| `--ki-video-play-radius` | `14px` | dimension used by the video component for the play container shape: Radius/component/radius_xl, the exact variable bound to the Size=md Icon_button instance (14px, the ki-icon-button xl radius). |
| `--ki-video-play-shadow` | `0 1px 1px -0.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 3px 3px -1.5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 20px 20px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 32px 32px -16px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 56px 56px -28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 72px 72px -36px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.03), 0 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)` | shadow used by the video component for the play control (MarsUI stacks Elevation/e6 on the button_wrap beneath the Component_effect/primary_default inner White/12 highlight — the measured master stack). |
| `--ki-video-play-size` | `3.5rem` | dimension used by the video component for the play container box: the 56px Icon_button instance of the Size=md Video (the ki-icon-button xl box; the sm variant's 40px box is the scale demo). |
| `--ki-video-scrim-color` | `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)` | color used by the video component for the overlay scrim behind the play control: Inverse_white/alpha_6, the exact variable bound to the Play halo on both Video variants (Black/5 light, White/5 dark) — the scheme flip rides the semantic layer. |
| `--ki-video-scrim-padding` | `1rem` | dimension used by the video component for the scrim clearance around the play container: Space/3xl, the exact variable bound as the Size=md Play halo padding (16px on every side). |
| `--ki-video-scrim-radius` | `28px` | dimension used by the video component for the overlay scrim shape: Radius/component/radius_6xl, the exact variable bound to the Size=md Play halo (28px pill-cornered square). |

## Preference modes

The tokens each mode reassigns, on top of the active theme. A theme that
sets one of them in its own rule replaces the mode value too, because both
rules declare the same property on the same element: restate it under the
mode's media query and declaration (see *Docs → Tokens & theming*). More
contrast also applies reduced transparency.

### reduced-transparency

Active under `@media (prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce), (prefers-contrast: more)` and on a token root carrying, or inside one
carrying, `[data-ki-transparency='reduce']` or `[data-ki-contrast='more']`.

| Token | Value under the mode (onmars) |
| --- | --- |
| `--ki-alert-shell-bg` | `var(--ki-surface-s1)` |
| `--ki-select-listbox-backdrop-blur` | `var(--ki-blur-none)` |
| `--ki-surface-danger-tint` | `var(--ki-surface-danger-base-em)` |
| `--ki-surface-info-tint` | `var(--ki-surface-info-base-em)` |
| `--ki-surface-primary-tint` | `var(--ki-surface-primary-low-em)` |
| `--ki-surface-special-blur-base-dark` | `var(--ki-surface-special-light-s0-dark-s3)` |
| `--ki-surface-special-blur-base-light` | `var(--ki-surface-special-light-s0-dark-s3)` |
| `--ki-surface-special-secondary-alpha-base` | `var(--ki-surface-special-light-s2-dark-s3)` |
| `--ki-surface-success-tint` | `var(--ki-surface-success-base-em)` |
| `--ki-surface-warning-tint` | `var(--ki-surface-warning-base-em)` |

### contrast-more

Active under `@media (prefers-contrast: more)` and on a token root carrying, or inside one
carrying, `[data-ki-contrast='more']`.

| Token | Value under the mode (onmars) |
| --- | --- |
| `--ki-alert-border-width` | `var(--ki-space-xxs)` |
| `--ki-alert-danger-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-alert-info-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-alert-neutral-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-alert-success-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-alert-warning-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-card-border-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-details-border` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-dialog-border` | `var(--ki-size-border-hairline) solid var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-dialog-divider-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-divider-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-focus-ring-width` | `var(--ki-shadow-ring)` |
| `--ki-image-fallback-fg` | `var(--ki-text-supporting)` |
| `--ki-indicator-dot-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-list-item-divider-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-outline-control` | `var(--ki-inverse-white-alpha-64)` |
| `--ki-outline-control-hover` | `var(--ki-inverse-white-alpha-72)` |
| `--ki-select-placeholder-fg` | `light-dark(var(--ki-text-supporting), var(--ki-inverse-white-alpha-80))` |
| `--ki-tabs-divider-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |
| `--ki-text-supporting` | `var(--ki-inverse-white-alpha-72)` |

| `--ki-tooltip-border-color` | `var(--ki-outline-strong)` |

## Deprecated tokens

Still published with their values until the version shown. Use the replacement
listed for each token (spec 041; see
`docs/migrations/deprecated-tokens.md`).

| Token | Replacement | Deprecated since | Removed in | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `--ki-alert-shell-backdrop-blur` | `--ki-alert-shell-backdrop-filter` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Use a complete filter value so a flat theme can turn compositing off. |
| `--ki-button-backdrop-blur` | `--ki-button-backdrop-filter` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Use a complete filter value so a flat theme can turn compositing off. |
| `--ki-button-ghost-active-shadow` | `--ki-button-ghost-rest-shadow` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: a pressed button keeps its resting shadow under the pressed overlay. |
| `--ki-button-ghost-danger-active-bg` | `--ki-button-ghost-danger-rest-bg` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: a pressed button shows its resting cell under the pressed overlay (--ki-button-secondary-active-overlay). |
| `--ki-button-ghost-danger-active-border` | `--ki-button-ghost-danger-rest-border` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: a pressed button shows its resting cell under the pressed overlay (--ki-button-secondary-active-overlay). |
| `--ki-button-ghost-danger-active-fg` | `--ki-button-ghost-danger-rest-fg` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: a pressed button shows its resting cell under the pressed overlay (--ki-button-secondary-active-overlay). |

| `--ki-dialog-backdrop-blur` | `--ki-dialog-backdrop-filter` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Use a complete filter value so a flat theme can turn compositing off. |
| `--ki-dialog-bg-end` | `--ki-dialog-bg-image` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Set the complete background image through the image token. |
| `--ki-dialog-bg-start` | `--ki-dialog-bg-image` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Set the complete background image through the image token. |

| `--ki-input-focus-border` | `--ki-input-focus-ring-color` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: a focused field keeps its resting border and is marked by its focus ring. |
| `--ki-input-min-target` | `--ki-input-height` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: the field's minimum block size is its height. |
| `--ki-motion-easing-spring` | `--ki-motion-easing-emphasized-out` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | The onmars curve now eases out without overshoot. |
| `--ki-select-hover-bg` | `--ki-surface-hover-overlay-inverse` | 0.1.0 | 1.0.0 | Nothing paints it: on hover the trigger keeps its resting fill under the inverse hover overlay. |
